Pdf Recall Online Editor Assessment Super Asset Assessment Assignment Class Opinion Hands-on Project Now once there Assets and the structural combinations are added to the program. The program is published and the published version is V1 and when this version is publish, there will be cohorts created and then learners for this cohort will be enrolled. Now when the learner is part of the jounrey the Content users can come and either Upgrade the asset which will upgrade to the learner but no updating the progress calculation or completed status of the asset to the learner. ChatGPT said:
Thanks for the detailed context! Based on the description of your LMS application, here’s a structured set of test scenarios for the "Upgrade" cases, covering both Assets and Programs. I'll separate them into asset-specific and program-specific scenarios and also include a combined section.
________________________________________
Test Scenarios for Asset Upgrade (Manual - Functional)
1. Upgrading Assets for Learners in Progress
•	
Scenario 1: Verify that when an asset is upgraded (e.g., PDF replaced, new assessment questions added), the updated content is reflected for the learner but their progress/completion status remains unchanged.
•	
•	
Scenario 2: Verify that if a learner has completed an asset before the upgrade, the "completed" status remains intact after the upgrade.
•	
•	
Scenario 3: Verify that for learners who have not yet started the asset, they only see the updated version after the upgrade.
•	
•	
Scenario 4: Verify that different asset types (e.g., PDF, Hands-on, Assignment) are upgraded successfully without affecting the structural combinations (Modules, Sessions, Segments).
•	
•	
Scenario 5: Verify that upgrading an asset for a cohort does not impact other cohorts using the same program.
•	
•	
Scenario 6: Verify the rollback mechanism (if applicable) for an asset upgrade to restore the previous version of the asset.
•	
2. Notifications & Logs
•	
Scenario 7: Verify that learners are notified (if applicable) about the asset upgrade in their cohort.
•	
•	
Scenario 8: Verify that upgrade actions are properly logged for audit purposes (e.g., who upgraded the asset, timestamp, and changes made).
•	
3. Edge Cases for Asset Upgrades
•	
Scenario 9: Verify that an asset upgrade with invalid content (e.g., corrupt file, invalid assessment configuration) does not impact learners and triggers appropriate error messages.
•	
•	
Scenario 10: Verify that simultaneous upgrades to multiple assets in the same segment do not cause any system inconsistencies or display issues for learners.
•	
________________________________________
Test Scenarios for Program Upgrade (Manual - Functional)
1. Changing Program Configurations
•	
Scenario 11: Verify that updating a program’s due dates reflects correctly for all enrolled learners in the respective cohort.
•	
•	
Scenario 12: Verify that learners with completed or in-progress statuses for the program are not impacted when configurations are updated.
•	
•	
Scenario 13: Verify that any configuration changes (e.g., module order, prerequisites) apply only to future progress tracking and not to the progress already recorded.
•	
•	
Scenario 14: Verify that changes to program configurations are reflected across all modules, sessions, and segments as intended.
•	
2. Notifications & Logs
•	
Scenario 15: Verify that learners are notified (if applicable) about program updates (e.g., due date changes).
•	
•	
Scenario 16: Verify that program update actions are logged for audit purposes.
•	
3. Edge Cases for Program Upgrades
•	
Scenario 17: Verify that simultaneous updates to program configurations (e.g., by two admins) result in proper conflict resolution or system validation.
•	
•	
Scenario 18: Verify that updates made to one cohort's program configuration do not affect other cohorts.
•	
________________________________________
Test Scenarios for Combined Upgrades (Assets and Programs)
1. Cohort-Level Impact
•	
Scenario 19: Verify that upgrading an asset within a program and simultaneously updating program configurations (e.g., due dates) does not cause conflicts or data inconsistencies.
•	
•	
Scenario 20: Verify that a learner consuming a program is seamlessly transitioned to the updated asset and program configurations without errors or data loss.
•	
•	
Scenario 21: Verify the system behavior when multiple assets and program configurations are upgraded in quick succession (e.g., ensure no learner-facing interruptions).
•	
2. Notifications
•	
Scenario 22: Verify that learners receive clear and accurate notifications when both an asset and the program are upgraded for their cohort.
•	
3. Edge Cases
•	
Scenario 23: Verify that when an asset is upgraded and later rolled back while program configurations are updated, the learner experience remains consistent with the latest valid data.
•	
•	
Scenario 24: Verify the behavior when an upgraded asset is part of multiple programs and one program is updated simultaneously.
•	
________________________________________
Additional Notes
1.	
Dependency Testing: Ensure that structural dependencies (Module → Session → Segment → Asset) are maintained during upgrades.
2.	
3.	
Cross-Cohort Testing: If learners in multiple cohorts use the same program, verify isolation between cohorts during upgrades.
4.	
5.	
Regression Testing: Include regression tests for learner progress tracking, notifications, and reporting dashboards.
6.

Sprint:	LXP_2024_S19
Epic Link:	Tracks and Electives 

 Description  	 
When I click on “Add Track Groups” available at the program level for each delivery type
Then I should be able to add Track Groups at each program level
Acceptance Criteria:
•	User should be able to see an action at program level as “Add Track Groups“
•	User should be able to add multiple track groups
•	On clicking “Add Track Groups“, a popup should be shown which should allow the user to add Track Groups 
o	Label - Mandatory
o	Group Name - Mandatory
•	On Save, a Track Group container should be created which should have the below options to 
o	Edit
o	Delete
o	Add Track
o	Add Track Selection and Available Date
•	On clicking Edit, the popup should open and allow the user to change Label and Group Name
•	On clicking delete, a confirmation popup should open with a message Heading - “Delete Track Group“, and Subheading - “Are you sure you want to delete the Track Group?“
•	On clicking Confirm, the track group should get deleted and on clicking Cancel, no action should happen
Design - https://www.figma.com/design/YFYqxb15NtTve45JWoUUaK/KH-Tasks?node-id=886-94140&t=WHineIbZYY7UUvLO-1

Sprint:	LXP_2024_S19
Epic Link:	Tracks and Electives 

 Description  	 
When I click on Add Tracks action inside track Group
Then I should be able to add multiple tracks inside a track group
Acceptance Criteria:
•	User should be able to see an action “Add Track“ for a track group
•	On clicking “Add Track“, a popup should be shown which should allow the user to add Track details with below fields -
Field Name	Field Type	Description	Mandatory/Optional
Label	text box	allows user to add label name	M
Track Name	text box	Allows user to add Track Name	M
Description	text area	allows user to add description	O
Not Applicable for Program Package key (PPK) IDs	text box - autosuggest multiple selectable tags	lists all the ppk ids, allows user to add multiple PPK ids	O
•	On Save, a Track container should be created which should have the below options to 
o	Edit
o	Delete
o	Add Course
•	On clicking Edit, the popup should open and allow the user to change Label and Track Name, description, PPK Ids fields.
•	On clicking delete, a confirmation popup should open with a message Heading - “Delete Track“, and Sub heading - “Are you sure you want to delete the Track?“
•	On clicking Confirm, the track group should get deleted and on clicking Cancel, no action should happen
•	Multiple addition of PPK ids should be allowed which on selection, the particular track should not be shown to the learner in the track group if this program curriculum is assigned for a learner
•	User should not have the option of setting selection, and available dates as they are dependent on Track Groups visibility
•	Once Track is added, user should be able to add multiple courses inside each track which should be as per current behaviour
Design - https://www.figma.com/design/YFYqxb15NtTve45JWoUUaK/KH-Tasks?node-id=546-25911&t=9pvlfIFcKgMBsPFC-1

Sprint:	LXP_2024_S21, LXP_2024_S19
Epic Link:	Tracks and Electives 

 Description  	 
When I want to change the default order of tracks
Then I should be able to rearrange the order of tracks and define the default order of tracks
Acceptance Criteria:
•	For every track group, one track will be a default track which will be automatically assigned to learner once track selection date is passed
•	Content Author/Manager, once adding the not available PPK ids, should be able to rearrange the tracks in a order
•	The tracks from top in the order should be considered as default for the learners as per the non availability of tracks for a PPK ids.
•	Example: 
o	Consider there are three tracks added for a track group - T1, T2, T3.
o	If T1 is marked as not available for PPK 1 and the order of Tracks defined is T1, T2, T3 where T2 and T3 are not marked not available for PPK 1
o	A learner is enrolled in a program of PPK 1
o	The learner will not see Track 1 and learner will not have this as default track and T2 will be the default track as it is next in the order from top.
Design - https://www.figma.com/design/YFYqxb15NtTve45JWoUUaK/KH-Tasks?node-id=546-26499&t=9pvlfIFcKgMBsPFC-1

Sprint:	LXP_2024_S19
Epic Link:	Tracks and Electives 

 Description  	 
When: I am on a Study plan where Track Groups and Tracks are available
Then: I should be able to see Track Groups and Tracks
Acceptance Criteria:
•	Learner should be able to see the track group in study plan as non-clickable until the Available date arrives showing Label and Group Name as per the configuration in CMS along with a tool tip message on hovering the info icon and the message should be as in the design
•	Once Track Selection date arrives, learner should be able to see a CTA “Choose Track“, which should open a popup and show all the available tracks inside that Track Group.
•	Learner should not be able to see the Track if any track is marked as not available for this program PPK ID.
•	On the popup, learner should be able to see the track available date mentioned in CMS with a message in the tooltip as in Figma design
•	Learner should be able to see a message on how default track will be assigned
•	Learner should be able to see all the available tracks inside track group and should be able to see all the content inside it but cannot open any asset
•	Learner should be able to see the “default track” tag for a track that is default
•	Learner should be able to see an option to select a track
Design - https://www.figma.com/design/YFYqxb15NtTve45JWoUUaK/KH-Tasks?node-id=521-20983&t=ZeTCLXXIrBqoqviJ-1

•	On clicking No, the track should not be changed

•	Electives can also be added inside a track group while adding course inside a track
•	Electives can be added at course levels. one elective is nothing but a course
•	On clicking “Add Elective Group“, a popup should be shown which should allow the user to add Elective Course details with below fields -
Field Name	Field Type	Description	Mandatory/Optional
Label	text box	allows user to add label name	M
Name	text box	Allows user to add Elective Group Name	M
•	On Save, an Elective Group container should be created which should have the below options to 
o	Edit
o	Delete
o	Add Elective
o	Add Elective Selection and Available Date
•	On clicking Edit, the popup should open and allow the user to change Label and Group Name and PPKs
•	On clicking delete, a confirmation popup should open with a message Heading - “Delete Elective Group“, and Sub heading - “Are you sure you want to delete the Elective Group?“
•	On clicking Confirm, the elective group should get deleted and on clicking Cancel, no action should happen
•	Elective group can be added inside a track as well

 Description

Then I should be able to add multiple elective courses inside an elective group

•	On clicking “Add Elective Course“, a popup should be shown which should allow the user to add Elective Course details with below fields -
Field Name	Field Type	Description	Mandatory/Optional

Sprint:	LXP_2024_S19
Epic Link:	Tracks and Electives 

 Description  	 
When I click on “Set Track Selection and Available Dates“ action on a Track Group
Then I should be able to add Track Selection and Available Dates for a track group
Acceptance Criteria:
•	User should be able to see an action “Set Track Selection and Available Date“ on Track Group
•	On clicking, the user should be able to see a popup with 2 fields “Track Selection Date“ and “Track Available Date“as per the design to add dates
Field Name	Field Type	Description	Mandatory/Optional
Track Selection Date	Calendar	Allows user to add Track Group Selection date for the learners	M
Track Available Date	Calendar	Allows user to add Track Group Available date for learner to consume	M
•	Track Group Selection date is the date from which the learners can select the track and until this date arrives, learners should not be able to choose tracks
•	Track Group Available date is the date from which the learners can access the content of the selected track
•	Learners should be able to change tracks until 7 days post Track Group Available dates

 [IRA-50495] As a Content Author/Manager, I should be able to rearrange Electives so that I can define the default elective by order Created: 13/Aug/24  Updated: 10/Dec/24 
Status:	In Production
Project:	IRA
Components:	None 
Affects versions:	None 
Fix versions:	28 Nov 2024 - UG Delta Production Release 
Parent:	Tracks and Electives

Type: 	Story 	Priority: 	High 
Reporter: 	Ahammad Gazali (Inactive) 
Assignee: 	Dipak Shaw 
Resolution: 	Unresolved 	Votes: 	0 
Labels: 	None 
Σ Remaining Estimate:	0 minutes 	Remaining Estimate:	Not Specified 
Σ Time Spent:	5 hours 	Time Spent:	Not Specified 
Σ Original Estimate:	Not Specified 	Original estimate:	Not Specified 

Issue links: 	Bug
has Bugs 	IRA-53619 
Elective group name is not showing in... 	Resolved 

Sub-tasks:	Key	Summary	Type	Status	Assignee
IRA-50588 
QA Story Testing IRA-49903 
Sub-task 	Done 	ramesh   
IRA-50589 
QA Test Case Drafting IRA-49903 
Sub-task 	Done 	Puja yellamelli   

Sprint:	LXP_2024_S21, LXP_2024_S19
Epic Link:	Tracks and Electives 

 Description  	 
When I want to change the default order of Electives (Elective is a course)
Then I should be able to rearrange the order of electives and define the default order of electives
Acceptance Criteria:
•	For every elective group, one elective will be a default elective which will be automatically assigned to learner once elective selection date is passed
•	Content Author/Manager, once adding the not available PPK ids, should be able to rearrange the electives in a order
•	The electives from top in the order should be considered as default for the learners as per the non-availability of electives for the PPK ids.
•	Example: 
o	Consider three electives added for a elective group - E1, E2, E3.
o	If E1 is marked as not available for PPK 1 and the order of electives defined is E1, E2, E3 where E2 and E3 are not marked not available for PPK 1
o	A learner is enrolled in a program of PPK 1
o	The learner will not see elective 1 and learner will not have this as default elective and E2 will be the default elective as it is next in the order from top.
Design - https://www.figma.com/design/YFYqxb15NtTve45JWoUUaK/KH-Tasks?node-id=546-26499&t=9pvlfIFcKgMBsPFC-1

 [IRA-58775] Content Author/Manager - Changes in Program Dashboard Created: 06/Mar/25  Updated: 24/Apr/25 
Status:	QA Inprogress

when the base program is not publishe... 	Resolved

QA Test Case Drafting IRA-58773

IRA-60694 
Issues of sync workshop for master pr... 
Sub-task 	Done 	jairaj   
IRA-60991 
Changes in Program Dashboard 
Sub-task 	Done 	saurabh.sagar   

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S6, LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8
Epic Link:	Program Versioning - Phase 1 

 Description  	 
Description
With this change in program versioning, each cohort of the program is supposed to have it’s own program curriculum and version history. This will also change how we present the programs on the content side. The changes are mentioned in the AC of the story.
Acceptance Criteria
1.	Currently, the program dashboard contains the following columns - program name, delivery type, variant, category name, status, and a 3 dots menu.
2.	We are changing the columns of the program dashboard as most of the columns have become irrelevant.
3.	The columns will now be - program name, curriculum id, number of cohorts, & latest published version. 
1.	Program Name - This column represents the name of the program.
2.	Curriculum ID - This column shows the curriculum id of the program which is used to communicate across systems such as OMS.
3.	Number of Cohorts. - This column shows the number of cohorts that are currently created for this program and is listed inside the base program.
4.	Latest Published Version - This column shows the latest published version of the latest cohort in that program curriculum.
4.	The program name on this table is clickable.
5.	Clicking on the program name shall take me to a page that shows the list of cohorts that are created for this program curriculum.
6.	In case the program is being published for the first time and there is no cohort attached as of now, show 0 in no.of cohorts column and in case there is no published version of the program as of then, show “-” in latest published version column.

IRA-61003 
Program's Cohort List Page 
Sub-task 	Done 	saurabh.sagar   

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S6, LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8
Epic Link:	Program Versioning - Phase 1 

 Description  	 
Description
Clicking on the program name shall take me to the list of cohorts that are using the same curriculum in order of their start date. This story describes that page where all the cohorts for that program are to be shown.
Design - https://www.figma.com/design/RWycQP1mBBzJ1eSugjMyn1/Asset-%26-Program-Versioning?node-id=1638-6948&t=8lNgmux42a7PLPbS-0 
Acceptance Criteria
1.	This page contains a static title - “Program Versions”, a close button at the top-right, the program curriculum details at the top, filters for the page, and the list of cohorts that are using this program curriculum.
2.	Clicking on the close button at the top right of the page shall close this page and take me to the program dashboard page.
3.	The curriculum details section above the cohort list table shall show the program name and the filters button.
4.	There are two filters here - cohort id and status. Figma: https://www.figma.com/design/RWycQP1mBBzJ1eSugjMyn1/Asset-%26-Program-Versioning?node-id=1612-5191&t=8lNgmux42a7PLPbS-0
5.	The filters popup has standard filter popup interaction.
6.	The cohort list table contains 4 columns and a 3 dots actions menu - 
1.	Cohort ID - This columns shows the cohort ids that use this program curriculum.
2.	Cohort Start Date - This column shows the cohort start date of the respective cohorts.
3.	Version - This column shows the current version of the program curriculum i.e being used by this cohort. This columns will always show the latest version applicable for this cohort. For e.g, the fifth cohort will have the version as 5.X where X is the latest version of that curriculum published for that cohort.
4.	Status - This column shows the status of the current version of the program curriculum applicable for that cohort.
7.	Please note this cohort list is to be sorted on the basis of Versions. The latest/most recent cohort or the latest version would be shown first followed by older versions. V10.X → V9.X → V8.X → …. V1.X
8.	Empty State: The case where a program has been created for the first time and no cohorts have been published for the program yet. 
1.	In case there are no cohorts created for a program yet, the cohort list will be shown as empty with only one row where the cohort id and the cohort status columns will show “-” since there is no cohort that is attached to the program curriculum yet.
2.	The version column will show 1.X where X is 0 if the curriculum was published only once or X>0 based on the number of times the program was published. Please note, cohorts might still not be attached to the program.
3.	The status column shall show the status of the program curriculum based on the editing status of the current versions.
9.	3-dots menu - The 3 dots menu against a cohort shall contain the same menu options as a program depending on the different statuses of the program. More details on the editing flow of the cohort in this story - https://upgrad-jira.atlassian.net/browse/IRA-58777
10.	This list of cohorts table has standard pagination interaction.

Program Versioning - Phase 1 (IRA-58773) 
 [IRA-58777] Content Author/Manager - Editing & Publishing journey for each cohort of a program Created: 06/Mar/25  Updated: 30/Apr/25 
Status:	QA Inprogress
Project:	IRA
Components:	None 
Affects versions:	None 
Fix versions:	25 Apr 2025 - UG Delta Production Release 
Parent:	Program Versioning - Phase 1

Type: 	Story 	Priority: 	High 
Reporter: 	Dipak Shaw 
Assignee: 	ramesh 
Resolution: 	Unresolved 	Votes: 	0 
Labels: 	None 
Σ Remaining Estimate:	0 minutes 	Remaining Estimate:	0 minutes 
Σ Time Spent:	1 day, 4 hours 	Time Spent:	Not Specified 
Σ Original Estimate:	0 minutes 	Original estimate:	0 minutes 

Issue links: 	Blocks
is blocked by 	IRA-60864 
the latest cohort curriculum is not a... 	Resolved 
is blocked by 	IRA-61439 
not able to see the all cohorts in co... 	Resolved 
is blocked by 	IRA-61742 
in program versioning, the program is... 	Resolved 
Bug
has Bugs 	IRA-61427 
The CMS end API calls is taking more... 	Resolved 
has Bugs 	IRA-61487 
when searching by program name worksh... 	Resolved 

Sub-tasks:	Key	Summary	Type	Status	Assignee
IRA-59061 
QA Story Testing IRA-58773 
Sub-task 	In Progress 	ramesh   
IRA-59062 
QA Test Case Drafting IRA-58773 
Sub-task 	Deferred 	  
IRA-61004 
Editing & Publishing journey for each... 
Sub-task 	Done 	saurabh.sagar   

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S6, LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8
Epic Link:	Program Versioning - Phase 1 

 Description  	 
Description
This story describes the editing and publishing flow of each of the cohorts that are present inside a program curriculum.
Acceptance Criteria
1.	Each program acts as a base program now, it will not have its own version but every time a cohort is created, a new program version would attached to that cohort.
2.	At first when a program is created, it will not have any cohorts created, please refer pt. 8 of this story for this use case.
3.	As soon as the first cohort is created, it will receive the first version or V1.0 as the curriculum version for that cohort. In case the program has already been updated a few times, it will receive the latest version of V1 which could be V1.2 or V1.3 or V1.X.
4.	In case the curriculum is currently in draft state and the first cohort is created, the cohort will receive the latest published version of the curriculum.
5.	As soon as a second cohort is created for the program, it will receive the last published version of the previous cohort as the base version for that cohort. For e.g, let’s say if the last published version of the first cohort was V1.7 then this will become V2.0 for the second cohort which means V2.0 = V1.7
6.	Now, I can edit V2.0 in case I want to make changes to the curriculum of the second cohort or I can go and edit V1.7 if I want to make changes to the first cohort.
7.	A new version V3.0 will be created as soon as a 3rd cohort is created from the OMS for this curriculum and so on.
8.	As a content author/manager, I shall be able to edit diff cohort’s curriculum simultaneously since their editing and publishing journey’s are diff.
9.	As a content author/manager, I will be able to edit only the latest published version of all the cohorts of a program curriculum. For e.g, if a program has 3 cohorts, I will be able to edit the latest version of each cohort, if cohort 1’s latest version 1.7, I will be able to edit V1.7 and not 1.6 or 1.3.
10.	As a content author/manager, I cannot create a new version of a program curriculum without launching a cohort, a new master version will only be created once a new cohort is launched from OMS.
11.	Please note there is no master version of a curriculum, it only flows forward i.e each cohorts last published version acts as the master version for the next cohort’s first version.
12.	The current flow for editing a program is applicable for each of the cohorts present inside the base program.
13.	There are a few changes as well that is applicable for editing a program - 
1.	The option “Edit Program” will now say “Edit Program Version”. This also means that editing a program version shall only edit that cohort’s program version and not any other cohort’s curriculum.
2.	As a content author/manager I shall see one more option in the 3-dots menu in addition to the already existing options i.e Discard Unpublished Version. This option will only appear for an unpublished curriculum version i.e if the status is in Draft, In-Review, Feedback Received, or Good to Publish.
3.	Cloning a program will now be available at a cohort level inside a curriculum. This means that cloning a program will create a clone of that cohort’s latest version be it in published or an unpublished state. For more details on the cloning behaviour of a cohort’s curriculum version, please refer to this story: https://upgrad-jira.atlassian.net/browse/IRA-58778
4.	The preview option has now become “Preview as Learner”. Clicking on this button shall open the program view for a learner of that cohort. All the dates for that cohort shall be shown as per that cohort’s dates.
5.	Please note, for the preview as learner option, if the dates for the cohort is not confirmed yet, the dates are to be shown as per the content start date as the content start date is by default the cohort start date.
6.	As mentioned earlier, there is one more option on the actions menu i.e discard unpublished version. For entire flow, please refer to this story: https://upgrad-jira.atlassian.net/browse/IRA-58780
14.	The publishing flow for a cohort will be same as it was available earlier, no change there.
15.	In case there are no cohorts published for a program, I can still keep editing the already published version. Once a cohort is launched from OMS, it will receive the latest published version of the cohort.
16.	The numbering of sub-versions for a cohort shall follow this pattern - 1.0, 1.1, 1.2, 1.3, …, 1.9, 1.10, 1.11, 1.12, etc.
17.	Please note, a new version shall not be created in case the content author/manager is changing the program configuration settings for a cohort, but the changes will only be applicable for that cohort and not other cohorts.

 Description  	 
Objective
As a Content Author/ Manager, I shall be able to view the history of versions published for a cohort’s curriculum. 
Design - https://www.figma.com/design/RWycQP1mBBzJ1eSugjMyn1/Asset-%26-Program-Versioning?node-id=72-1193&t=0Af5TPop6FyWnaW0-0

3.	Published By - This shows the email of the content manager who published the program. The second row in this column also shows the date and time of when this version was published.
4.	Drafted By - This shows the email of the content author/manager who moved the program to draft. The second row in this column also shows the date and time of when this version was moved to draft.
3.	This table contains standard pagination and interaction.
4.	Hovering over a field which is truncated shall show the full text.
5.	This page also contains a close button at the top-right of the page. Clicking on this close button shall close this page and take the user to the cohort list page of that program.

We are extending this capability for the authors/managers to be able to copy containers from different cohorts of the same program as well.
Design - https://www.figma.com/design/RWycQP1mBBzJ1eSugjMyn1/Asset-%26-Program-Versioning?node-id=1693-13569&t=6CxXkCAalp2YkXku-0

2.	The list that comes after clicking on the clone/copy option on the 3-dots menu of a container shall contain all cohort level versions of the same program as well. 3.	I shall see multiple rows for ACP Gen AI with diff rows signifying the latest versions of the respective cohort level curriculum.
4.	The delivery type column shall be replaced on this table with Cohort ID since one program can have multiple cohorts and diff curriculums. It does not not make sense to show delivery type column here.
5.	Please note, I will only be able to copy containers from the latest published version of a cohort curriculum and not the previous versions.
6.	Filters - I shall see one more option in filters i.e Cohort ID. I shall be able to filter the list of programs by cohort id as well.

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S6, LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8
Epic Link:	Program Versioning - Phase 1 

 Description  	 
Description
As a program reviewer, I see the list of programs and there are two options in the menu - View Published Version and View Latest Version. This program dashboard will change based on the program versioning changes.
Acceptance Criteria
1.	The program dashboard will receive the same design changes as the content author/manager role.
2.	The program name would be clickable. Clicking on the program name would show the list of cohorts that are using that program curriculum. The columns for this page would remain same for program reviewer as well.
3.	The 3 dots menu on the program reviewer role would still have two options - “View Published Version” & “View Latest Version”.
4.	Clicking on any one of the options shall not trigger a popup since it will show the dates of the containers based on the content start date/cohort start date of the cohort. This logic is already mentioned in this story: https://upgrad-jira.atlassian.net/browse/IRA-58783

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S6, LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8
Epic Link:	Program Versioning - Phase 1 

 Description  	 
Description
Currently, a list of courses is displayed on the phoenix platform while deferring a learner. This list of courses depend on the program curriculum as of now but this behaviour is changing after program versioning where each cohort will have its own program curiculum.
Acceptance Criteria
1.	As a deferrals manager, when I am deferring a learner from ug learn to prism, I see a list of courses which is to be selected for progress and score transfer. This list will not be impacted as it shows all the courses of the source cohort which would from ug learn side.
2.	There is another touchpoint where the deferrals manager needs to provide mapping for the courses selected for score and progress transfer. This will be impacted as the mapping b/w source target cohort’s courses now depends on the target cohort’s curriculum and not on the program curriculum.
3.	The course mapping shall follow the existing logic i.e if mapping b/w source and target cohort’s courses, the deferral manager cannot or does not need to map the courses but in case the mapping does not exist, they have to provide the mapping while reviewing the request.


 Comments  	 
Comment by Dipak Shaw [ 09/Apr/25 ] 
Vishnuprasad Santi - Please note that since each cohort will have it’s own curriculum, the list of courses that is shown while providing the course mapping on phoenix shall show based on the target cohort’s curriculum only and not based on the 1st or base cohort of that program curriculum.

Sub-task 	Deferred 	  

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S6, LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8
Epic Link:	Program Versioning - Phase 1 

 Description  	 
Description
Currently, the view program as a learner features does not depend on the cohort but now a program as well has multiple cohorts so when I click on the view as a learner option on the cohort page from any role, it should show that cohort’s curriculum and not any other cohort’s curriculum.
Roles that have “view as a learner”:
1.	Delivery Manager
2.	Cohort Manager
3.	Buddy
Acceptance Criteria
1.	For all the roles mentioned above, I shall see the respective cohort’s curriculum and not any other cohort’s curriculum when I click on “View as a Learner” option for a cohort.
2.	The dates for all the containers in the preview shall follow the content start date/ cohort start date for that cohort.
3.	Clicking on the view as a learner from these three roles shall always show the latest published version of the cohort curriculum and not the one in a non-published state.
4.	The delivery manager has another tab where we are showing the option to configure career centre settings and learner profile Qs at a program curriculum level. This tab will also show the program dashboard as per the content author side.
5.	The program name would be clickable and clicking on the program name will display the cohort list. Please note, the columns on the program dashboard and cohort list page would be same here as that of content author/manager.
6.	The 3-dots menu against the cohort curriculum would have the same options as before - “Career Centre Settings” and “Manage Learner Profile”. Editing these options shall only impact the cohort for which I am editing these settings and not other cohorts.

Program Versioning - Phase 1 (IRA-58773) 
 [IRA-59090] Program Versioning - Impact on Learner's side Created: 10/Mar/25  Updated: 29/Apr/25 
Status:	QA Inprogress

is blocked by 	IRA-61585 
Incorrect Cohort Version Display in T... 	Resolved 

Sub-tasks:	Key	Summary	Type	Status	Assignee
IRA-59092 
QA Story Testing IRA-58773 
Sub-task 	In Progress 	ramesh   
IRA-59093 
QA Test Case Drafting IRA-58773 
Sub-task 	Deferred 	  
IRA-61014 
Impact on Learner's side 
Sub-task 	Done 	saurabh.sagar   

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S6, LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8
Epic Link:	Program Versioning - Phase 1 

 Description  	 
Description
There is no impact of program versioning on the learner’s side. All the changes are to be reflected for the learners of that cohort and not of other cohorts.
Acceptance Criteria
Progress & Scores
1.	The learner’s progress and scores of the learners on the existing assets shall not be impacted due to a change in program versioning.
2.	In case an asset is deleted, only then the learner’s progress and scores will be lost.
3.	In case a new asset is added to the program, the learner will have to attend the asset for them to get progress and scores in that asset.
Scorecard
1.	The scorecard on the learner side shall be updated based on the changes in the new version update of that cohort’s curriculum
Academic Calendar
1.	The academic calendar on the learner side shall be updated based on the changes in the new version update of that cohort’s curriculum.
Help & Support
1.	All tickets created by the student shall contain program version info along with the program name on the support ticket.
2.	Please note there are 3 places from which a ticket can be created, all touchpoints to be covered here.
Communications
1.	All communication triggers shall be updated based on the updated dates and deadlines of the containers/assets in the program.
Tracks & Electives
1.	Please note if the learner has already selected the tracks and electives applicable for them then they should not require to select the tracks and electives again.
Dates & Deadlines
1.	The dates and deadlines for all the containers/assets in the cohort curriculum would need to be recalculated based on the changes to the cohort curriculum.
GIO
1.	In case changing the program structure changes the eligibility of the learner for a GIO then the learner shall be able to take or not take the GIO. Existing functionalities of GIO shall work here.


 Comments  	 
Comment by Dipak Shaw [ 29/Apr/25 ] 
Sanganabasu Hulagabal jairaj We agreed, no need to send program versions in support ticket but asset versions will still be sent.
CC: ramesh

Generated at Sat May 10 18:43:30 UTC 2025 by Kishore Murkhanad using Jira 1001.0.0-SNAPSHOT#100283-rev:b313437bee33f337bb713e94b166455345e8bdee. 


Introducing GIO Courses at program andcourse levels, enabling respective managers to handle eligibility, extensions, and enrolment.

Creation and Cloning of GIO Courses

Enable the creation of GIO Courses at course or program level from scratch.

Facilitate cloning GIO Courses from other programs or within the same program.

GIO Course Configuration Options

Define grading distributions (weightage, cutoffs) for GIO Courses at both program and cohort levels.

Set eligibility and scoring rules (Lower/Upper Eligibility Range, Score Capping) for both percentage and GPA systems.

Seamless Management and Visibility

Let Delivery/Cohort Managers manage due dates, grading, and extension availability for all GIO courses.

Provide easy bulk operations (score uploads, penalty revisions).

Flexible Enrollment and Extensions

Grant Enrollment Managers the ability to directly enroll learners into GIO Courses, bypassing standard eligibility checks if needed.

Allow for enabling/disabling extension availability at both program and cohort levels.

Enhanced Learner Experience

Allow learners to see GIOs in their study plan when eligible or enrolled.

Integrate GIO scores into the learner’s scorecard.

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S5, LXP_2025_S6, LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8
Epic Link:	Introducing GIO Courses at program andcourse levels, enabling respective managers to handle eligibility, extensions, and enrolment. 

 Description  	 
•	Given the Content Author/Content Manager is creating a Program
•	When they click on the three-dot menu for the Program → ‘Create GIO Course’ → ‘Create a New Course’ in the pop-up
•	Then the user should be able to create a GIO as a new course from scratch.
Acceptance Criteria:
1.	When the user clicks on the three-dot menu for the program, they should see ‘Create GIO Course’ as an option. Design: https://www.figma.com/design/uWKhJXclxWIMKbch8BiGlA/GIO-Standardisation?node-id=62-788&t=QgaGFUbPXgQMzsfo-4
2.	When the user clicks on Create GIO Course, a pop-up should open with an option ‘Create a New Course’. Design: https://www.figma.com/design/uWKhJXclxWIMKbch8BiGlA/GIO-Standardisation?node-id=20-1905&t=QgaGFUbPXgQMzsfo-4
3.	When clicked, a course should be created with a Label ‘Program GIO’ displayed.
4.	When the user clicks on the three-dot menu beside the Program GIO, the user should see a Delete option.
5.	When View is clicked, the user should be able to add Modules, Assets, set available/due dates, and clone modules from other programs, just like normal courses.
6.	One a Course GIO is created for a Program user should not be able to create another GIO or GIO Course.
7.	Program GIO is shown at the bottom after all the courses

 Comments  	 
Comment by Nikhil Yalagonda [ 26/Mar/25 ] 
While creating a GIO course, show a popup asking for Course Name, Course Label, and Description (as is the current behavior).
However:
•	If the GIO course is being created at the course level, pre-fill the popup fields by fetching the Course Name, Label, and Description from the parent course. The Content Manager should still be able to edit these fields.
•	If the GIO course is being created at the program level, show the same popup but with empty input fields, allowing the Content Manager to enter the details manually.
Comment by Nikhil Yalagonda [ 28/Mar/25 ] 
Gaurav Mantan If a Course has a GIO Course and if a user tries to delete the Course then and error should be shown saying “This course can’t be deleted because it’s linked to a GIO course.”

Epic Link:	Introducing GIO Courses at program andcourse levels, enabling respective managers to handle eligibility, extensions, and enrolment. 

 Description  	 
•	Given the Content Author/Content Manager is creating a Program
•	When they click on the three-dot menu for the program → ‘Create GIO Course’ → ‘Clone a course from another program’ in the pop-up
•	Then the user should be able to navigate through other programs, clone courses (including selecting/deselecting modules, sessions, assets), and mark it as a Program-level GIO.
Acceptance Criteria:
1.	When the user clicks on the three-dot menu for the program, they should see ‘Create GIO Course’ as an option.
2.	When the user clicks on Create GIO Course, a pop-up should open with an option ‘Clone a course from another program’. Design: https://www.figma.com/design/uWKhJXclxWIMKbch8BiGlA/GIO-Standardisation?node-id=20-1801&t=QgaGFUbPXgQMzsfo-4
3.	When clicked, a new page listing all existing programs should appear, allowing the user to browse and clone a course (select/deselect modules, sessions, segments, and assets, per existing behavior).
4.	After this new course is created, the user should be able to modify the GIO Course.
5.	When the user clicks on the three-dot menu beside the GIO, they should see a Delete option.
When View is clicked, the user should be able to add Modules, Assets, set available/due dates, and clone modules from other programs, just like normal courses.

o	A GIO assignment/project already exists for that course, or
o	Another GIO Course is already present.
6.	If any GIO is present (either project/assignment or GIO Course), “Create GIO Course” is hidden.
When View is clicked, the user should be able to add Modules, Assets, set available/due dates, and clone modules from other programs, just like normal courses.

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S5, LXP_2025_S6, LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8
Epic Link:	Introducing GIO Courses at program andcourse levels, enabling respective managers to handle eligibility, extensions, and enrolment. 

 Description  	 
•	Given the Content Author/Content Manager is creating a Program
•	When the user clicks on the three-dot menu beside any course in Tracks, Electives, or Electives within Tracks
•	Then they should be able to ‘Create GIO Course’.
Acceptance Criteria:
1.	When the user clicks on the three-dot menu beside any course in Tracks, Electives, or Electives within tracks, they should see ‘Create GIO Course’ as an option.
2.	When clicked, a pop-up with these options should appear: 
o	Create a New Course
o	Clone a Course from Another Program
o	Clone this Course
3.	Clicking ‘Create a New Course’ creates a new course and marks it as GIO.
4.	Clicking ‘Clone a Course from Another Program’ allows the user to clone and mark it as GIO.
5.	Clicking ‘Clone this Course’ clones the same course and marks it as GIO for the same course.
6.	All created GIOs can be deleted by clicking the three-dot menu beside them → Delete.
7.	All created Course-as-GIO can be modified just like a normal course.

Epic Link:	Introducing GIO Courses at program andcourse levels, enabling respective managers to handle eligibility, extensions, and enrolment. 

 Description  	 
•	Given a user has created a GIO for a course/program
•	Then the user should not be able to create another GIO inside that GIO.

3.	A course/program cannot have multiple GIOs.
4.	A course/program cannot have multiple GIO Courses.
5.	A course/program cannot have both a GIO (asset) and a GIO Course simultaneously.

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S5, LXP_2025_S6, LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8
Epic Link:	Introducing GIO Courses at program andcourse levels, enabling respective managers to handle eligibility, extensions, and enrolment. 

 Description  	 
•	Given the user is on the ‘Program Configuration Page’
•	When the user clicks on ‘Define Distribution for GIO Courses’ (a new section)
•	Then the user should see all the GIO Courses along with their graded components and be able to assign weightage and cutoff.
Acceptance Criteria:
1.	The user should see all GIO Courses under ‘Define Distribution for GIO Courses’.
2.	The user should see all graded components inside each GIO Course.
3.	The user can create Additional Components for the GIO Course.
4.	The user can assign weightage and cutoff (in %) for each component inside the GIO Course.
5.	Learner score at course level should be calculated based on the weightage allocated to each Component
For Example: Let the following be the weightage of different Components and a learners scores
Component	Weightage	Score
C1	25%	50%
C2	25%	60%
C3	50%	100%
Then:
Here's the step-by-step calculation:
C1:
Weightage = 25%
Score = 50%
Contribution = 25% × 50% = 0.25 × 50 = 12.5%
C2:
Weightage = 25%
Score = 60%
Contribution = 25% × 60% = 0.25 × 60 = 15%
C3:
Weightage = 50%
Score = 100%
Contribution = 50% × 100% = 0.50 × 100 = 50%
Final Course Percentage:
Sum of contributions = 12.5% + 15% + 50% = 77.5%

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S5, LXP_2025_S6, LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8
Epic Link:	Introducing GIO Courses at program andcourse levels, enabling respective managers to handle eligibility, extensions, and enrolment. 

 Description  	 
ption:
•	Given the user is on ‘Program Configuration’ Page with Percentage scoring selected
•	When the user clicks on ‘GIO Score Configuration’ section
•	Then the user can set these values for all GIOs and GIO Courses.
Acceptance Criteria:
1.	The user can set Lower Eligibility Range and Upper Eligibility Range (in %).
2.	The Lower Eligibility Range accepts values between 0 and 99.
3.	The Upper Eligibility Range accepts values between 1 and 100.
4.	Upper Eligibility Range must be greater than Lower Eligibility Range, and both fields are required.
5.	If a learner’s score is between Lower (inclusive) and Upper (inclusive), they are eligible for the GIO. And the eligible learner should see the GIO course in the Study Plan.
6.	For existing GIOs, set Lower Eligibility Range = 0.
7.	If a learner’s score exceeds ‘Score Capped at’, it is capped to that value (e.g., 60% → 50% if capping is 50%).
8.	If a learner’s score is below the capping value, their original score is retained.
A note at the bottom should read: “Both Lower Eligibility Range and Upper Eligibility Range are included when checking eligibility.”

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S5, LXP_2025_S6, LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8
Epic Link:	Introducing GIO Courses at program andcourse levels, enabling respective managers to handle eligibility, extensions, and enrolment. 

 Description  	 
•	Given the user is on the Program Configuration Page
•	When the user scrolls to the GIO section
•	Then they can enable or disable extensions for GIO at the program level.
Acceptance Criteria:
1.	The user can see ‘Enable Extension for GIOs’ under the GIO section on the first page of Program Configuration. Design: https://www.figma.com/design/uWKhJXclxWIMKbch8BiGlA/GIO-Standardisation?node-id=42-173&t=QgaGFUbPXgQMzsfo-4
2.	By default, this is disabled.
3.	Users can enable it, which enables extensions beyond the due date for learners.
4.	For all existing Programs, it remains disabled by default.
5.	If this is enabled then learner should be able to extend due date for Graded assets Modules in the Program
6.	If a learner extends the due date then the Penalty rules should be applicable accordingly based on the configuration set at program level.
7.	When a learner extends a due date then the number of extensions available for the learner should decrease by 1 at program level.

 Description  	 
Description:
•	When I click on Study Plan
•	Then I should see the GIOs I’m eligible for. Design: https://www.figma.com/design/uWKhJXclxWIMKbch8BiGlA/GIO-Standardisation?node-id=71-9625&t=QgaGFUbPXgQMzsfo-4
Acceptance Criteria:

4.	The learner also sees a Program-level GIO if they are eligible.
5.	For Course-level GIO and Program-level GIO, eligibility is checked per the configured Lower/Upper Eligibility Range.
6.	On clicking the GIO, the learner can attempt/submit it.
The GIO course runs in parallel to other courses and does not affect their timelines or sequencing.

Epic Link:	Introducing GIO Courses at program andcourse levels, enabling respective managers to handle eligibility, extensions, and enrolment. 

 Description  	 
When I am creating or editing a program → Program Configuration → GIO Score Configuration → Final Score Card Configuration,
Then I should be able to configure whether GIO scores are the final scores or if the final score should be determined as the best of the two (Original Course Score and GIO Score).
Design: https://www.figma.com/design/uWKhJXclxWIMKbch8BiGlA/GIO-Standardisation?node-id=260-464&t=ECCxc1XHjjnQhagh-4 
Acceptance Criteria:

o	Set GIO scores as Final Scores
o	Set the Best of GIO and Original Scores as Final Scores
•	An info icon should be displayed next to the title ‘Final Score Card Configuration’, and when hovered over, the following tooltip message should appear:
"Choose how the final scores are determined for the program: 
o	Set GIO scores as Final Scores: The GIO scores will be used as the final scores, regardless of whether they are higher or lower than the learner's original course scores. (This is the default selection.)
o	Set the Best of GIO and Original Scores as Final Scores: The final scores will be the higher of the two scores—either the GIO score or the original course score."
o	Design: https://www.figma.com/design/uWKhJXclxWIMKbch8BiGlA/GIO-Standardisation?node-id=266-897&t=ECCxc1XHjjnQhagh-4
•	The selected configuration should reflect in the learner's final scorecard.

IRA-58944 
QA Test Case Drafting IRA-58491 
Sub-task 	Deferred 	  

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S5, LXP_2025_S6, LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8
Epic Link:	Introducing GIO Courses at program andcourse levels, enabling respective managers to handle eligibility, extensions, and enrolment. 

 Description  	 
GIO is an internal terminology and the naming can differ for universities so we need to give flexibility for Content Authors/Managers to set different namings at program level which will reflect on learner study plan and scorecard.
When I am creating/editing a Program, in the program configuration page, inside GIO Score Configuration Section 
Then I should be able to set the naming for GIOs which will reflect on the learner Studyplan and scorecard based on University requirements.
Acceptance Criteria: 
1.	I should see three input fields with labels  Design: https://www.figma.com/design/uWKhJXclxWIMKbch8BiGlA/GIO-Standardisation?node-id=260-470&t=ECCxc1XHjjnQhagh-4 
1.	GIO Score Card Title
2.	Course GIO Tag
3.	Program GIO Tag
2.	The input in in GIO Score Card Title name will replace the title of GIO scorecard on learner end. Design: https://www.figma.com/design/uWKhJXclxWIMKbch8BiGlA/GIO-Standardisation?node-id=73-12244&t=ECCxc1XHjjnQhagh-4
3.	The input in Course GIO Tag will be shown for all GIO courses as a Tag in Learner Studyplan & Score card. Design: https://www.figma.com/design/uWKhJXclxWIMKbch8BiGlA/GIO-Standardisation?node-id=266-7149&t=ECCxc1XHjjnQhagh-4
4.	The input in Program GIO Tag will be shown for all GIO courses as a Tag in Learner Studyplan & Score card.
5.	An tooltip with following information is shown with following information when user hovers over the info icon beside “GIO Score Card Label” title 
1.	What are these GIO labels for?
These fields control the text shown to learners when they have a GIO. 
	GIO Scorecard Title: Appears as the main heading on the GIO scorecard (e.g., “Resit Scorecard,” “Supply Scorecard”).
	Course‐Level GIO Label: Shown next to a specific course if the learner is completing a GIO for that course (e.g., “Resit,” “Supplementary Attempt”).
	Program‐Level GIO Label: Shown if the learner has a GIO for an entire program (e.g., “Supplementary Program Attempt”).
Example in action
Let’s say a university calls GIOs “Resits.” You could enter:
•	GIO Scorecard Title: “Resit Scorecard”
•	Course GIO Tag: “Course Resit”
•	Program GIO Tag: “Program Resit”
From the learner’s perspective, any course that’s part of a GIO would be labeled “Course Resit” in their study plan and scorecard, and the entire GIO section would be headed by “Resit Scorecard.” If the learner had to redo all courses in a program, they would see “Program Resit” next to those courses.

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S5, LXP_2025_S6, LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8, LXP_2025_S9
Epic Link:	Introducing GIO Courses at program andcourse levels, enabling respective managers to handle eligibility, extensions, and enrolment. 

 Description  	 
When I click on Skill Report,
Then I should see up to three report cards in the tabbar based on the program configuration and my GIO attempts.
Types of Score Cards:
•	Original Score Card: Displays scores from different courses without any reattempts or GIOs. Design: https://www.figma.com/design/uWKhJXclxWIMKbch8BiGlA/GIO-Standardisation?node-id=73-18751&t=ECCxc1XHjjnQhagh-4
•	*GIO Score Card {Name is dynamic and configured while creating the Program } 
:* Appears alongside the Original Score Card if the learner has attempted at least one GIO. This scorecard includes only GIO & GIO Course scores. Design: Design: https://www.figma.com/design/uWKhJXclxWIMKbch8BiGlA/GIO-Standardisation?node-id=73-18754&t=ECCxc1XHjjnQhagh-4
•	Final Score Card:  
o	If the Content Manager configures “Score Card with GIO”, this scorecard displays all course scores and GIO scores replace course scores if the user attempted any.
o	If the Content Manager configures “Set the Best of GIO and Original Scores as Final Scores”, this scorecard displays the higher of the two scores for each course.
o	Design: https://www.figma.com/design/uWKhJXclxWIMKbch8BiGlA/GIO-Standardisation?node-id=71-5737&t=ECCxc1XHjjnQhagh-4
Acceptance Criteria:
•	When I click on Skill Report,
Then I should see the three report cards based on the program configuration and my GIO attempts: 
o	Original Score Card (always available).
o	*GIO Score Card {This name is set at configuration page while creating/editing the program} 
* (if at least one GIO has been attempted).
o	Final Score Card (if configured by the Content Manager and at least one GIO is attempted). Design:
•	If I do not attempt any GIOs, then tabbar should disappear and course scores should be displayed
•	If I attempt at least one GIO and the Content Manager has configured “Set the Best of GIO and Original Scores as Final Scores”, then the Best Achieved Scores should be displayed in the Final Score Card along with other course scores.
•	If I attempt at least one GIO and the Content Manager has configured “Set GIO Scores as Final Scores”, then all the GIO Scores along with other course scores should be displayed in the Final Score Card.
•	Final Score card and GIo Score card will be shown to then learner when any GIO scores are published for the GIOs the learner has taken.
•	The following are different cases and what score cards and logic to be utilised while showing Scorecards. Then:
•	Case 1: Learner did not attempt any GIO 
o	Show only original score card and hid Tab Bar
•	Case 2: Learner has attempted a Course GIO and GIO scores as Final scores is set at program Level 
o	Show all 3 score cards
o	Final Score Card will have all courses and courses with GIOs will be replaced by GIO/GIO Course Scores
o	Final Score Card will be default score card
o	GIO Score card will only have GIO/GIO course scores.
o	The Final scorecard and GIO Score Card(The score card name is dynamic and set while creating the program) will be enabled when atleast one GIO scores are published
•	Case 3: Learner has attempted a Course GIO and Set the Best of GIO and Original Scores as Final Scores is set at program Level. Then: 
o	Show all 3 score cards
o	In the Final Score Card, any course with a GIO attempt will show the better (higher) score between the original score and the GIO score.
o	Final Score Card will be default Score Card
o	The Final scorecard and GIO Score Card(The score card name is dynamic and set while creating the program) will be enabled when atleast one GIO scores are published
•	Case 4: Learner attempts a Program GIO with or without attempting any Course GIOs and GIO scores as Final scores is set at program Level. Then: 
o	Show all 3 score cards
o	Final Score Card will only have Program GIO scores
o	Final Score Card will be default Score Card
o	GIO Score Card will have all GIOs, GIO Courses and Program GIOs scores.
•	Case 5: Learner attempts a Program GIO with or without attempting any Course GIOs and Set the Best of GIO and Original Scores as Final Scores is set at program Level. Then: 
o	Show all 3 score cards
o	In the Final Score Card: 
	For each course, display the higher (best) score between the original course score and any GIO attempt.
	If the score of Program GIO is greater than original and course GIOs combined then showcase only Program GIO score. Design: https://www.figma.com/design/uWKhJXclxWIMKbch8BiGlA/GIO-Standardisation?node-id=266-8952&t=ECCxc1XHjjnQhagh-4
o	Final Score Card will be default Score Card
o	The Final scorecard and GIO Score Card(The score card name is dynamic and set while creating the program) will be enabled when atleast one GIO scores are published

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S5, LXP_2025_S6, LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8, LXP_2025_S9
Epic Link:	Introducing GIO Courses at program andcourse levels, enabling respective managers to handle eligibility, extensions, and enrolment. 

 Description  	 
Acceptance Criteria:
•	When an asset appears in both the original course and a GIO,
Then the system should allow the learner to attempt it again under the GIO context, even if it was previously completed.
•	When an asset is included in both the original course and GIO,
Then completion status should be tracked separately.
•	When the learner completes the asset in the original course but not in the GIO,
Then the asset should remain unattempted (black icon) in the GIO study plan.
•	When an asset is a duplicate in both Original Course and GIO,
Then it should not contribute to module or course-level progress in GIO.
•	When a learner attempts the asset in GIO,
Then the new score should be recorded separately in the GIO-specific scorecard, without overwriting the original course score.
•	The original course completion data and scores should remain intact and separate from GIO attempts.

Deferral Enhancements - Phase 2 (IRA-59790) 
 [IRA-59853] Learn to Prism - Credits Scorecard - Removing criteria to match gpa in target cohort Created: 01/Apr/25  Updated: 23/Apr/25 
Status:	Dropped
Project:	IRA

Sub-task 	ToDo 	  
IRA-60339 
QA Test Case Drafting IRA-59790 
Sub-task 	ToDo 	  

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S7
Epic Link:	Deferral Enhancements - Phase 2 

 Description  	 
Description
Currently, there are two restrictions on the prism side for deferrals -
1.	Max gpa on the source cohort shall be equal to that of the target cohort
2.	Course level gpa scored by the learner in the source cohort shall have a grade letter in the target cohort
We are removing both the restrictions from deferrals since the same is being removed from the content manager side as well. 
Acceptance Criteria
1.	Both the above mentioned criteria shall be removed for the cgpa to cgpa deferrals only if university provided gpa is selected as a setting in the target cohort’s scorecard.
2.	Please note we are also removing the grade letter to grade point configuration on prism for university provided gpa. This change will only work after that step from scorecard is removed otherwise it might break for the learner.
3.	In this case, we will fetch both the learner’s grade point and grade letter from the source cohort on learn and pasting it in the target cohort on prism.
4.	Please note, all other touch points for this type of deferrals shall also be checked and verified. That includes multiple roles such as Delivery Manager & Learner. The behaviour for them would be same as that of regular slab wise deferrals.

 [IRA-59854] Prism to Prism - [Phoenix] Selection of courses for score and progress transfer Created: 01/Apr/25  Updated: 06/May/25 
Status:	QA Inprogress
Project:	IRA

Sub-tasks:	Key	Summary	Type	Status	Assignee
IRA-60338 
QA Story Testing IRA-59790 
Sub-task 	ToDo 	  
IRA-60341 
QA Test Case Drafting IRA-59790 
Sub-task 	ToDo 	  

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8
Epic Link:	Deferral Enhancements - Phase 2 

 Description  	 
Description
Currently, if i am deferring a learner from a prism cohort to another, the entire progress and scores of the learner is inherently transferred to the target cohort. The team wants control over this, they want to transfer the learner without progress and grades and sometimes, with progress and grades in some courses but not all. Hence, we are enabling a similar solution for prism to prism deferrals as that of learn to prism.
Workflow
User enters learner mail id -> Selects source & target cohorts -> Select courses for progress & score transfer (if applicable) -> Raise a deferral request -> Review the request and provide course mapping b/w source and target cohorts -> Approve the request -> Complete payment calculation and pay the amount (if applicable)
Acceptance Criteria
1.	After entering the email id of the learner on phoenix, I shall get the option to select the source & target cohort on phoenix, no changes here.
2.	After selecting the source cohort, I shall also get the list of courses from the source cohort to select for progress and grades transfer. Currently, this is not coming on phoenix.
3.	The list of courses shall contain all the courses from the source cohort, including the ones that do not have graded assets or is marked as optional.
4.	Please note, I shall be able to select any course from the list for progress and grades transfer. The restriction for being able to select only those courses that have grades published is relaxed here. This is done for allowing progress transfer for courses that do not have graded assets or is marked as optional.
5.	Please note, selecting the progress and grade transfer check box is mandatory for the transfer. In case, the courses are selected but these flags are not, save changes button shall be disabled with error message “You have selected courses but not enabled the progress and/or grade transfer flag”.
6.	The course names in this list shall come with the track/elective name in case it is applicable for the course. For more details, please refer to this story: https://upgrad-jira.atlassian.net/browse/IRA-55684
7.	Similarly, if the flags are selected but no courses are selected, the save changes button shall be disabled with error message “Please select courses for progress and/or grade transfer to proceed”.
8.	Once all the values are filled properly, clicking on the save changes button shall raise a deferral request, no change in behaviour here, same as learn to prism.
9.	Please note, this behaviour is same for both % to % deferrals and cgpa to cgpa deferrals (prism to prism).


 Comments  	 
Comment by Dipak Shaw [ 02/May/25 ] 
ramesh Atul Yadav Ravi Tatikonda - As discussed in the group, we are removing grade and progress transfer flags for prism to prism deferrals, so no need to disable save changes button. Aligned on this. 
CC: Alka Bahirat

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8
Epic Link:	Deferral Enhancements - Phase 2 

 Description  	 
Description
After raising the deferral request, the user has the option to edit the courses selected for progress and score transfer and to provide the mapping of courses for the learner, similar to learn to prism deferrals.
Acceptance Criteria:
1.	After raising the request on phoenix, the user goes to the review request tab to take action on the request, this is same as that of the learn to prism behaviour.
2.	Clicking on the take action button against that deferral request shall open the request in a side panel, existing behaviour.
3.	On this side panel, currently we do not get the option for edit courses or edit course mapping for prism to prism deferral requests, this is to be enabled here as well.
4.	Clicking on the edit courses button shall show the list of courses selected for progress and grades transfer and I shall be able to edit this as well, same as that of learn to prism deferrals.
5.	[Edge Case] For pt.4, if user deselects all the courses that he selected for progress and score transfer here, the button to proceed shall be disabled with an error message “Please select at least one course”.
6.	Clicking on the edit course mapping button shall show the popup for providing the source to target course mapping.
7.	In case the mapping is already present, it shall be populated here and I shall be able to edit the mapping as well.
8.	Please note, for both the “edit courses” and “edit course mapping” popup, I shall see the track/elective names in the same way as learn to prism deferrals if it is applicable for the course.
9.	Similar to learn to prism, the next steps would be followed and the payment amount is to be calculated and the deferral request shall be approved/rejected on phoenix.
10.	Please note, this behaviour is same for both % to % deferrals and cgpa to cgpa deferrals (prism to prism).
11.	In case a deferral was successful but not all grades were transferred, a remark shall be shown on the phoenix side along with the success status - “Some of the grades were not transferred due to curriculum change, please check and upload manually if required.”
12.	On the step to provide course mapping for the learner, if the user has selected courses belonging to multiple tracks or electives in the same track or elective group, the button to proceed shall be disabled along with an error message “You have selected courses belonging to multiple tracks/electives in one group”

Deferral Enhancements - Phase 2 (IRA-59790) 
 [IRA-59858] Prism to Prism - Backend Functionalities & Logic Created: 01/Apr/25  Updated: 09/May/25 
Status:	QA Inprogress
Project:	IRA
Components:	None 
Affects versions:	None 
Fix versions:	25 Apr 2025 - UG Delta Production Release 
Parent:	Deferral Enhancements - Phase 2

Type: 	Story 	Priority: 	High 
Reporter: 	Dipak Shaw

Labels: 	None 
Σ Remaining Estimate:	0 minutes 	Remaining Estimate:	Not Specified

has tasks 	IRA-60706 
Prism to Prism - Backend Functionalit... 	Done 

Sub-tasks:	Key	Summary	Type	Status	Assignee
IRA-60343 
QA Story Testing IRA-59790 
Sub-task 	ToDo 	  
IRA-60344 
QA Test Case Drafting IRA-59790 
Sub-task 	ToDo 	  
IRA-60706 
Prism to Prism - Backend Functionalit... 
Sub-task 	Done 	Vivekanandan Dureddy   

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8
Epic Link:	Deferral Enhancements - Phase 2 

 Description  	 
Description
This story discusses all the functionalities and logics that are to be followed for prism to prism deferrals. The key scenarios are:
1.	% to % deferral
2.	cgpa to cgpa deferrals 
1.	university provided gpa
2.	slab wise gpa
Acceptance Criteria
1.	Progress and grade transfer flags on phoenix will be functional for prism to prism deferrals, not selecting this would mean no progress and score transfer for the learner.
2.	If any of the two flags are selected, then the user needs to select the courses for progress and score transfer. This is given as a part of UI validation on the phoenix side.
3.	In case the selected course for transfer is an optional course or is a course that does not graded assets, only the progress shall be transferred since grades are not applicable here but the deferrals shall still be allowed.
4.	Progress for the non-graded assets in the selected courses for transfer would be marked as 100%.
5.	Grades transfer would be done at an asset level. For e.g, let's say that you are deferring a learner from C1 to C2 and have selected one course for progress and score transfer which contains 5 graded assets, the scenarios are: 
1.	All 5 graded assets of the source cohort are present in the target cohort - scores for each asset will be transferred.
2.	Only 3 graded assets of the source cohort are present in the target cohort - scores for those 3 will be transferred but for the remaining 2 assets, the grades will be lost. 
3.	All 5 graded assets of the source cohort are diff in target cohort - no grades will be transferred.
6.	Please note, in all the sub-cases of pt.5, we will allow the deferral to succeed but will not be able to transfer the grades for all graded assets.
7.	The graded asset of the source cohort shall only be searched for in the mapped course of the target cohort and not in the entire curriculum. For e.g, if the course "Intro to Gen AI" of C1 was mapped to "Intro to LLM" of C2, we will check the presence of the 5 graded assets of "Intro to Gen AI" in only "Intro to LLM" and no other course.
8.	If the asset is present in any other course, progress and grades shall not be moved to the other course which was not mapped to the course in the source cohort.
9.	In case a deferral was successful but not all grades were transferred, a remark shall be shown on the phoenix side along with the success status - “Some of the grades were not transferred due to curriculum change, please check and upload manually if required.”
10.	Assets for which scores were transferred will be locked for re-attempt, existing behaviour for learn to prism deferrals but for the assets where scores were not transferred will follow the target cohort’s rules of deadlines and attempts and shall be allowed to attempt for the learner in the target cohort if applicable.
11.	Please note, we will transfer penalties as well along with the asset level scores, if any.
12.	In case the version of the asset is changing from V1 to V2 in the target cohort then his progress and scores will be retained in the target cohort but the learner will not be able to view the last attempt or submission. Please note, we are transferring the learner's progress and score from V1 to V2 so the learner will see the V2 of the asset in the target cohort and not the V1.
13.	Please note, in case there is a score for the learner in any other components such as class participation, exam score or additional components then that shall also be transferred for the selected courses.
14.	In case it is not present for the selected courses, I shall be able to upload the same for the selected courses for transfer from the grading panel on the delivery manager side.
15.	Please note, all of the logics are applicable for both % to % deferrals and cgpa to cgpa deferrals.
16.	Since we are removing the condition to check max gpa and grade letter condition for university provided gpa, the same change is applicable here as well. But the condition for slab wise gpa is to remain same as that of learn to prism deferrals.
17.	Please note, % to cgpa and cgpa to % deferrals are still not possible.
18.	The criteria to not allow to select courses belonging to multiple tracks/electives still remain for prism to prism deferrals.


 Comments  	 
Comment by Dipak Shaw [ 09/Apr/25 ] 
Vivekanandan Dureddy Adding an additional point for versioning in non-graded assets:
1.	In case the version of a non-graded asset is changing from source to target cohort, progress shall still be marked as 100% for the new version of the asset and we will show the new version of the asset to the learner. For e.g, if V3 was present in source and V4 was present in target, we will mark the progress as 100% for V4 in target and show the same to the learner.

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8
Epic Link:	Deferral Enhancements - Phase 2 

 Description  	 
Description
Deferring a learner from one prism cohort to another will have a similar effect on the delivery side as learn to prism deferrals.
Acceptance Criteria
1.	As a delivery manager or other sub-roles of delivery manager like cohort manager, I shall be able to upload the scores of the additional components(class participation, exam score, additional comp.) for all the courses of the deferred learner irrespective of whether the grades for that course was transferred or not.
2.	The above point is true for both percentage and CGPA version of the scorecard i.e I shall be able to upload the scores for these type of deferred learners irrespective of the scorecard version - % or cgpa.
3.	Extensions and the option to remove penalty shall be disabled for these learners on the assets where the score was transferred from another cohort.

QA Story Testing IRA-59790 
Sub-task 	ToDo 	  
IRA-60348 
QA Test Case Drafting IRA-59790 
Sub-task 	ToDo 	  

Sprint:	LXP_2025_S7, LXP_2025_S8
Epic Link:	Deferral Enhancements - Phase 2 

 Description  	 
Description
This story discusses the impact of prism to prism deferrals on the learner side. Most of the behaviour would remain same but since we are transferring the learner’s grades at an asset level, I shall be able to see the same on the scorecard as well.
Acceptance Criteria
1.	Study Plan 
1.	The graded assets(assessments, assignments, projects) for which the score was transferred would be available for the learner to view but they cannot see the last submission or attempt. They will just be able to see the score.
2.	For the ones where grade transfer could not happen due to curriculum change, the learner will see the asset as per the target cohort’s rules and deadlines. If the deadline has not passed yet, he shall be able to attempt, if already passed, the learner will not be able to attempt that asset.
3.	For all the non-graded assets in the course, the progress would be marked as 100%(deemed to be deferred) for the courses which was selected on phoenix for progress and grade transfer.
2.	Scorecard 
1.	The scorecard on the learner side shall show the scores as applicable for the learner.
2.	The scorecard shall still follow the rules of the target cohort i.e if the scores of all the assets of a component are not updated, the component level score will remain locked but on expanding the component, I shall be able to see the asset level score.
3.	Similarly, if any component level score is not updated for the learner, I shall not be able to see the course level score but on expanding the course, I shall be able to see where the scores are updated.
4.	Hovering over the “i” icon on the learner side shows the raw scores for a regular learner but for a deferred learner, it shall show a static text “Deferred” instead of the raw scores.
5.	The red “i” icon displayed beside a score where penalty was applied will be shown here as we are also transferring the penalty for the learner.
6.	Clicking on an asset shall still take the learner to asset report page but on the asset report page, I shall not be able to see the attempt on assessment or the submission file for assignments and projects.
3.	Live Sessions 
1.	Deferred learner will also see the same live session recordings as other learners in the cohort, no difference. For the past sessions which the deferred learner did not attend, the status shall show “Not Attended”.
2.	I shall still be able to attend all the upcoming sessions for the program and the track and elective that is selected for me.
3.	We will show the attendance of the learner in the previous cohort on the live session page but that is out of scope for this feature, it will covered in a separate requirement.
4.	Penalties 
1.	We are carrying forward the penalty from source to target cohort. The same is to be shown to the learner as well.
2.	As a result, we are also showing the penalties icon on the scorecard for the learner.
5.	Track & Elective Selection 
1.	Track or elective will be selected by the user deferring a learner by selecting the correct course of the track and elective.
2.	Track/Elective selection would follow the target cohort’s rule i.e if the track selection window has closed then as a learner, I shall not be able to select the track/elective but if the window is still open then I shall be able to.
3.	All the content/live sessions shall be loaded for the learner based on the track and electives selected for them.
6.	Extensions 
1.	Extensions are not carried forward from the source cohort, it will also follow the target cohort’s guidelines
7.	Re-Eval 
1.	As a learner, I shall not be able to raise a re-eval request for the assets where the score was transferred from a different cohort.
8.	GIO 
1.	If learner has failed any course in the source cohort and is eligible for GIO in the target cohort, it should work for them like regular learners.
9.	Other Touchpoints 
1.	If the number of assets have changed from the source to target cohort, delivery team is to work with content team to get the relevant scores uploaded manually for the learner. This does not concern the system.

Epic Link:	Deferral Enhancements - Phase 2 

 Description  	 
Description
As a learner if I am being deferred from learn to prism, certain details like program name and business unit are not being captured. We are enabling this info to be sent to OMS to maintain data hygiene and integrity.
API: 
Acceptance Criteria
1.	Learner shall see the program name that is being sent from OMS.
2.	Prism shall store the other meta data (Business Unit, Region) sent by OMS for internal uses as stored for the regular enrolment from OMS.
3.	Prism shall support these values to be accepted from a platform perspective.
